{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Adversarially review SIGIL Phase 7 WS-D \"cross-platform + mobile bridge\" (branch phase7-ws-d-mobile at /home/kali/sigil). The keystone is a device-authorization ledger that WIDENS the approval trust root (a phone's own key can now approve A2/A3 items) — attack it hard. Report substantiated findings with concrete repros, most severe first. Read the files fully.\n\nFILES:\n- sigil/mesh/registry.py (authorize_device/revoke_device/authorized_devices; advertise_capability/capability_map; DeviceApprover)\n- sigil/agents/approvals.py (the EXTENDED verify_approval + pending with extra_pubkeys — the trust-widening change)\n- sigil/bridge/daemon.py (BridgeDaemon: pending/submit_device_approval/panic_engage/relay; bind_ok)\n- sigil/bridge/notifier.py (PushNotifier — the {seq,tier,kind} push)\n- sigil/platform/{base,linux,macos,windows,secrets}.py\n- tests/test_mobile.py (greenwashed?)\n\nCLAIM 1 (HIGHEST) — a device can approve an A2/A3 item ONLY while it is OWNER-authorized, and the widened verify_approval still pins trust to OWNER-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2) and binds the signed target_seq (RP-APPROVAL-4). Attack:\n- Can a device self-authorize (sign its own device_authorized with its own key) and then approve? (test claims no — verify.) Can an UNSIGNED/forged device_authorized record authorize a device? Can a device authorized then REVOKED still approve? Can a device present a `pubkey` NOT in the authorized set and still pass verify_approval (the pub-in-allowed check)?\n- Replay: can a genuine device approval of a harmless seq be replayed onto a dangerous seq (target_seq binding)? Can a device approval signed for the OWNER's trusted key slot be confused?\n- Does authorized_devices / capability_map ignore an advertisement/authorization whose `pubkey` field doesn't match the actual signing key, or a tampered core field (verify_signed over the core)? Latest-wins per device — can an old authorize override a newer revoke by record ordering?\n- The daemon submit_device_approval: any bypass? Does it re-check authorization AND signature AND is the appended record actually resolved by pending() (not just accepted)? Does supersedes_id or a mismatched target_seq let it resolve the wrong item?\n\nCLAIM 2 — no secret or private subject crosses the tunnel/spine/log. Attack:\n- PushNotifier.poll: does the push EVER include the subject/text/payload? Only {seq,tier,kind}? Does it push A0/A1 (must not) or only queued A2/A3? Does the tailer event's `text` (which can contain the subject) leak into the push?\n- SecretStore: does set() ever write the secret to the spine or a log? Is the env-file 0600? Does get() prefer keyring? Any path where a secret is appended to the append-only spine?\n\nCLAIM 3 — bind doctrine + platform honesty. Attack:\n- bind_ok: does it refuse 0.0.0.0, ::, and public addresses, and allow loopback + private/WG? Edge cases: hostnames (not IPs), IPv4-mapped IPv6, link-local, a public IP that looks private, empty string.\n- panic_engage: is an UNSIGNED engage from a phone acceptable (fail-safe halt) — and can a phone RELEASE (it must NOT — release is the dangerous direction)? Confirm release is not exposed to the phone.\n- platform backends: do they return honest None on unavailability, never a fabricated Frame? Any offense (they must be observe-only)? Does the macOS/Windows backend import cleanly on Linux (lazy native imports)?\n- relay: is it gated (KernelDispatch → WARDEN), or does it execute arbitrary commands?\n\nFor each objection: file:line, concrete repro, severity, fix hint. If a claim holds, say what you tried. Return findings; do not fix."},"uuid":"fdd0041c-1ab7-43b7-ad7d-1cdc4f86b0d6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:18.778Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"fdd0041c-1ab7-43b7-ad7d-1cdc4f86b0d6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"<command-message>crucible</command-message>\n<command-name>crucible</command-name>\n<skill-format>true</skill-format>"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main/.claude/skills/crucible\n\n# CRUCIBLE — operating the engine\n\nCRUCIBLE turns an authorized seed URL into **oracle-confirmed** findings, reasons over them,\nand produces auditable reports — inside a fail-closed safety stack. Its whole value is one\nrule; internalize it before anything else:\n\n> **A claim is a FACT only when a deterministic oracle fires over data a real target\n> produced.** You (the LLM) ADVISE where to look and what a result might mean; the oracle\n> CONFIRMS. Nothing else — not your confidence, not a critic's endorsement, not a plausible\n> story — promotes a claim to a fact.\n\nThe standing doctrine every reasoning call operates under is\n[framework/cognitive/metacognition.md](../../../framework/cognitive/metacognition.md) (it is\ninjected into every LLM system prompt). The full architecture — agents, event spine, veracity\nlayer, learning/metacognition core, safety stack — is\n[framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md](../../../framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md). The binding\nconstitution is [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md); never relax scope, destruction, evidence, or\nhonesty rules.\n\n## The invariants (never violate these)\n\n1. **Oracle authority.** Only a fired oracle confirms. Critics/RL/reflection/self-consistency\n   may only *advise, re-rank, defer, or abstain* — never promote a finding or override the oracle.\n2. **Coverage.** Never silently skip an authorized attack surface. Learning and reflection\n   *deprioritise* (re-rank / defer); they never gate a surface out.\n3. **Prove by re-execution.** A finding is a fact only if its retained `oracle_context`\n   re-fires. The veracity firewall (`framework/v2/veracity/`) re-runs proofs; it can only demote.\n4. **Non-circular learning.** An EXPLOITABLE label needs ≥2 independent corroborating oracle\n   kinds; a silent oracle is never auto-labelled a false positive.\n5. **Refuse honestly, fail closed.** Decline to conclude what you cannot ground; record every\n   refusal as evidence. Hard limits (scope, authorization, destruction, real user data) are\n   inviolable — when unsure, stop and ask.\n6. **Determinism + append-only.** No wallclock/global-rng in learning/reward math; the event\n   spine is append-only (supersede, never edit).\n\n## Structured workflows\n\n### Run an authorized engagement (end-to-end, gated)\n1. Confirm the active target's `targets/<slug>/charter.md` authorizes it (constitution §II).\n2. `python3 -m framework.v2 engage <slug> <seed-url> [--recon] [--spine]`\n   — crawls, audits, confirms via oracles, chains attack paths, scores confidence, and\n   (with `--spine`) mirrors the whole run onto the immutable blackboard event stream.\n   (`--strict-evidence` is a **`scan`**-only flag, below — not an `engage` flag.)\n3. Every request passes the full gate chain; an out-of-scope seed or tripped kill-switch\n   refuses *before* traffic and is recorded as a `refusal` event.\n\n### Loopback-only quick scan\n`python3 -m framework.v2 scan http://127.0.0.1:<port>/ --format json|sarif|html [--strict-evidence]`\n— the export states each finding's **live grounding** (fact / ungrounded / contradicted);\n`--strict-evidence` withholds non-fact findings from the rendered doc but keeps them in\n`--reverifiable-out`.\n\n### Re-verify a report (prove-don't-guess, offline)\n`python3 -m framework.v2 verify <report.json>` re-runs each finding's retained `oracle_context`.\n`python3 -m framework.v2 evidence ...` builds/verifies signed, hash-linked evidence certificates\n(and, with report claims bound in, fails closed when a report sentence has no backing evidence).\n\n### Inspect / audit the event spine\nThe blackboard (`framework/v2/agents/blackboard.py`) is the one append-only, typed, provenance-\nlinked event stream. Replay it with `Blackboard.replay(engagement=..., since_id=...)`.\nCryptographic tamper-evidence: `framework/v2/agents/spine_chain.py`\n(`build_spine_chain` / `sign_spine_head` / `verify_spine_head`).\n\n### Recon / intel, memory, self-improvement\n`python3 -m framework.v2 intel ...` (OSINT into the shared world-model; gated egress),\n`memory ...` (cross-engagement priors — never fabricate a score), `improve ...`\n(authorise-not-apply — it never self-applies a change).\n\n### Read-only operator console\n`python3 -m framework.v2 console` — loopback, read-only, zero-impact operator UI over the run.\n\n## What NOT to do\n- Do not build detection-evasion / identity-rotation / stay-hidden-from-defenders capability —\n  CRUCIBLE is correlatable and authorized by doctrine (constitution §VI).\n- Do not let any learned/critic/LLM signal enter the deterministic oracle/SCE/calibration inputs\n  or promote a finding.\n- Do not attack third parties (payment/IdP/CDN) — test only the operator's integration with them.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"uuid":"d9f3fecc-2361-4bee-b537-5482f3151883","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:18.793Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"1e297b33-97cf-4f82-aae1-8c6fb67e718b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MbM7EM6eV1WAoAc2rQATuq","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"c503edab-0ac1-45f9-933f-bd81ce69dcad","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:37.688Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1e297b33-97cf-4f82-aae1-8c6fb67e718b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"949f0912-d25b-4016-a5bb-d3271e87411c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01P9UuEqpm6i28c3bhiFtrBN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\")\n27\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n28\t\n29\t\n30\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n31\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n32\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n33\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n34\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"])}\n35\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t\n39\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n40\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n41\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n42\t    latest: dict = {}\n43\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n44\t        p = r.payload\n45\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n46\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n47\t    return latest\n48\t\n49\t\n50\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n51\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n52\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n53\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n54\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n58\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n59\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n60\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n64\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n65\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n66\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n67\t    state: dict = {}\n68\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n69\t        p = r.payload\n70\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n71\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n72\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n73\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n74\t\n75\t\n76\tclass DeviceApprover:\n77\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n78\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n79\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n80\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n81\t        self.store = store\n82\t        self.device_key = device_key\n83\t\n84\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n85\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n86\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n87\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n88\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n89\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n90\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n91\t\n92\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n93\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n94\t\n95\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n97\t"}]},"uuid":"ee545450-28c9-4974-ab14-e130c8144c34","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:38.183Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"949f0912-d25b-4016-a5bb-d3271e87411c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"e83e65d8-b543-4b2a-9ed2-ed68860205ab","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA5sGQmuEXQGkL32TKhB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HUWWWJ4pZuH4mMy6a9SPdr","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/bridge/daemon.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5838,"cache_read_input_tokens":27020,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5838,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA5sDacjAjwDnoEK64Yf","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"f0eece83-66c5-40de-87c9-f84bc4fd7291","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:43.464Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"f0eece83-66c5-40de-87c9-f84bc4fd7291","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HUWWWJ4pZuH4mMy6a9SPdr","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"BridgeDaemon (Phase 7, WS-D D-v) — the desktop-side API the phone talks to over WireGuard. It\n2\treuses the owner-signed cores: the queue (`approvals.pending` with the authorized-device set), a\n3\tDEVICE-signed approval (accepted only while that device is authorized), a PANIC halt (any engage is\n4\tfail-safe), and a KERNEL command relay (`KernelDispatch` → the same WARDEN gate as voice/UI).\n5\t\n6\tBinding doctrine: the transport must bind the WireGuard interface (owned devices, encrypted tunnel)\n7\tor loopback — NEVER 0.0.0.0 / a public address. `bind_ok` enforces that; the HTTP transport reuses\n8\tthe WS-C server pattern bound to a `bind_ok` address.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport ipaddress\n12\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef bind_ok(addr: str) -> bool:\n18\t    \"\"\"True iff `addr` is safe to bind: loopback or a PRIVATE (e.g. WireGuard) address — never\n19\t    0.0.0.0 / a public / an unspecified address.\"\"\"\n20\t    try:\n21\t        ip = ipaddress.ip_address(addr)\n22\t    except ValueError:\n23\t        return False\n24\t    if ip.is_unspecified:                          # 0.0.0.0 / :: → refuse\n25\t        return False\n26\t    return ip.is_loopback or ip.is_private\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tclass BridgeDaemon:\n30\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n31\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n32\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n33\t        # the owner trust anchor — the persisted identity by default (injectable for tests)\n34\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n35\t\n36\t    def _authorized(self):\n37\t        from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n38\t        return authorized_devices(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey)\n39\t\n40\t    def pending(self) -> List[dict]:\n41\t        \"\"\"The phone-facing queue — minimal fields only (no subject leaks over the tunnel).\"\"\"\n42\t        from ..agents.approvals import pending\n43\t        items = pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=self._authorized())\n44\t        return [{\"seq\": r.seq, \"tier\": r.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": r.kind} for r in items]\n45\t\n46\t    def submit_device_approval(self, payload: dict) -> int:\n47\t        \"\"\"Append a DEVICE-signed approval the phone produced — ONLY if the signing device is\n48\t        currently authorized AND the signature verifies (fail-closed). A rogue/revoked device or a\n49\t        forged signature is refused; the signed target_seq binds (no replay).\"\"\"\n50\t        from ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n51\t        from ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n52\t\n53\t        class _Rec:\n54\t            def __init__(self, p): self.payload = p\n55\t        authorized = self._authorized()\n56\t        if payload.get(\"signal\") != APPROVAL_SIGNAL:\n57\t            raise ValueError(\"not an approval record\")\n58\t        if payload.get(\"pubkey\") not in authorized:\n59\t            raise ValueError(\"signing device is not authorized\")\n60\t        if not verify_approval(_Rec(payload), self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized):\n61\t            raise ValueError(\"approval signature invalid\")\n62\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n63\t                                 payload={**payload, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n64\t                                 supersedes_id=payload.get(\"target_seq\"))\n65\t\n66\t    def panic_engage(self, *, by: str = \"phone\") -> int:\n67\t        \"\"\"Halt the mesh from the phone. ANY engage halts (fail-safe) — no signature needed for the\n68\t        SAFE direction. Release stays owner-only at the desktop (the dangerous direction is signed).\"\"\"\n69\t        from ..governor.killswitch import SIGNAL as KS_SIGNAL\n70\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n71\t                                 payload={\"signal\": KS_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\", \"by\": by,\n72\t                                          \"reason\": \"panic halt from phone\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n73\t\n74\t    def relay(self, text: str) -> str:\n75\t        \"\"\"Relay a KERNEL command — the same T0-router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as voice/UI.\"\"\"\n76\t        from ..voice.dispatch import KernelDispatch\n77\t        return KernelDispatch().send(text)\n78\t"}]},"uuid":"9f2a9c5d-0ada-4fda-82ae-37bcb37c1a56","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:43.486Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f0eece83-66c5-40de-87c9-f84bc4fd7291","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"28b5a593-742b-4f2b-9255-cc53dc3e79e1","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA5sGQmuEXQGkL32TKhB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TifzK1zVCrG8ykHNPmUpWt","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/bridge/__init__.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5838,"cache_read_input_tokens":27020,"output_tokens":286,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5838},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":286,"cache_read_input_tokens":27020,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5838,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5838,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA5sDacjAjwDnoEK64Yf","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"c1abe4c8-3b49-45fd-b295-1a723c8de127","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:44.196Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"28b5a593-742b-4f2b-9255-cc53dc3e79e1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0139VjwbaMbK9ZUwKjJ6cbUG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"PushNotifier (Phase 7, WS-D D-vi) — pushes to the phone when an A2/A3 item QUEUES, fed by the\n2\tSHARED P0 `SpineTailer` (the same primitive the UI SSE feed uses). The push carries ONLY\n3\t`{seq, tier, kind}` — never the subject, never the payload, never a secret — so nothing sensitive\n4\tcrosses even the WireGuard tunnel; the phone fetches any detail on demand over the daemon API. No\n5\tcloud push (that would egress the private subject off owned hardware).\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n11\tfrom ..spine.tail import SpineTailer\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tclass PushNotifier:\n15\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, since_seq: Optional[int] = None):\n16\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n17\t        start = since_seq if since_seq is not None else self.store.next_seq - 1\n18\t        self.tailer = SpineTailer(self.store, since_seq=start)\n19\t\n20\t    def poll(self) -> List[dict]:\n21\t        \"\"\"New pushes since the last poll: one per newly-QUEUED A2/A3 item, minimal fields only.\"\"\"\n22\t        out: List[dict] = []\n23\t        for ev in self.tailer.poll():\n24\t            rec = self.store.get(ev[\"seq\"])\n25\t            p = rec.payload if rec else {}\n26\t            if (p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == \"awaiting-approval\"\n27\t                    and p.get(\"tier\") in (\"A2\", \"A3\")):\n28\t                out.append({\"seq\": ev[\"seq\"], \"tier\": p.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": ev[\"kind\"]})   # no subject/secret\n29\t        return out\n30\t"}]},"uuid":"177de413-66f0-480e-bc1b-5601c94660eb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:44.237Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"28b5a593-742b-4f2b-9255-cc53dc3e79e1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"c1abe4c8-3b49-45fd-b295-1a723c8de127","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TifzK1zVCrG8ykHNPmUpWt","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL mobile bridge (Phase 7, WS-D D-v/vi) — the phone as an owner-authorized remote-control +\n2\tapproval surface over WireGuard. The engine stays on the owned desktop; the phone approves (with its\n3\town authorized device key), panic-halts, and relays KERNEL commands. Push carries only {seq,tier,kind}\n4\tover the tunnel — no subject, no secret, no cloud. Offense-free.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n6\t\n7\tassert_no_offense()\n8\t\n9\tfrom .daemon import BridgeDaemon, bind_ok  # noqa: E402\n10\tfrom .notifier import PushNotifier  # noqa: E402\n11\t\n12\t__all__ = [\"BridgeDaemon\", \"PushNotifier\", \"bind_ok\"]\n13\t"}]},"uuid":"5f876ead-8596-4236-8887-ec09b2bbe721","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:44.240Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c1abe4c8-3b49-45fd-b295-1a723c8de127","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"5f876ead-8596-4236-8887-ec09b2bbe721","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA5sgFV33xuH1YAgnyJU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MALQwwFvJbu9Cw2tAUwqeG","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/platform/base.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3028,"cache_read_input_tokens":32858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3028,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":36,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA5scxnmcvBttmVuatmy","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"150a91d6-150d-4941-bbb1-8256ae0ee2fe","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:48.889Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"150a91d6-150d-4941-bbb1-8256ae0ee2fe","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MALQwwFvJbu9Cw2tAUwqeG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The host-backend contract (Phase 7, WS-D). A `HostBackend` captures screen/camera into the same\n2\t`Frame` the perception stack already consumes, and advertises what the host can do. The capability\n3\tdescriptor is what the mesh routes on (see `sigil.mesh`): which host has a camera, a GPU for a local\n4\tVLM, and is always-on — so vision/compute go to the best-capable ONLINE host while authority stays\n5\tin the one signed spine.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n9\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..perception.capture import Frame\n12\t\n13\t\n14\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n15\tclass CapabilityDescriptor:\n16\t    host_id: str\n17\t    os: str\n18\t    has_screen: bool\n19\t    has_camera: bool\n20\t    has_gpu_vlm: bool          # a local moondream-class VLM is reachable (Ollama up)\n21\t    always_on: bool\n22\t\n23\t    def to_dict(self) -> dict:\n24\t        return {\"host_id\": self.host_id, \"os\": self.os, \"has_screen\": self.has_screen,\n25\t                \"has_camera\": self.has_camera, \"has_gpu_vlm\": self.has_gpu_vlm, \"always_on\": self.always_on}\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@runtime_checkable\n29\tclass HostBackend(Protocol):\n30\t    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]: ...\n31\t    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]: ...\n32\t    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor: ...\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t# --- shared, OS-agnostic probes ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n36\tdef host_id() -> str:\n37\t    \"\"\"A stable per-host id (persisted once under SIGIL_HOME). Identifies this host in the mesh.\"\"\"\n38\t    import uuid\n39\t\n40\t    from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n41\t    f = SIGIL_HOME / \"host_id\"\n42\t    try:\n43\t        v = f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n44\t        if v:\n45\t            return v\n46\t    except OSError:\n47\t        pass\n48\t    SIGIL_HOME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n49\t    hid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]\n50\t    try:\n51\t        f.write_text(hid, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n52\t    except OSError:\n53\t        pass\n54\t    return hid\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef probe_gpu_vlm() -> bool:\n58\t    \"\"\"True iff a local moondream-class VLM endpoint (Ollama) is reachable — cheap TCP probe.\"\"\"\n59\t    import os\n60\t    import socket\n61\t    from urllib.parse import urlparse\n62\t    u = urlparse(os.environ.get(\"OLLAMA_HOST\", \"http://127.0.0.1:11434\"))\n63\t    try:\n64\t        with socket.create_connection((u.hostname or \"127.0.0.1\", u.port or 11434), timeout=0.5):\n65\t            return True\n66\t    except OSError:\n67\t        return False\n68\t\n69\t\n70\tdef probe_always_on() -> bool:\n71\t    import os\n72\t    return os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ALWAYS_ON\", \"\").lower() in (\"1\", \"true\", \"yes\")\n73\t"}]},"uuid":"bfbc59f9-e677-44c5-8c43-6f04be74e7cb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:48.933Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"150a91d6-150d-4941-bbb1-8256ae0ee2fe","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"bfbc59f9-e677-44c5-8c43-6f04be74e7cb","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA5sgFV33xuH1YAgnyJU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NVWChwC9RzQi8eWxnubB5F","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/platform/linux.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3028,"cache_read_input_tokens":32858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3028,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":36,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA5scxnmcvBttmVuatmy","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"4abb3769-8d8f-421c-aadd-d6ce807882a0","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:49.356Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"4abb3769-8d8f-421c-aadd-d6ce807882a0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NVWChwC9RzQi8eWxnubB5F","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Linux backend (Phase 7, WS-D) — wraps the existing perception capture verbatim (behaviour-\n2\tpreserving; the whole stack above is unchanged).\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport shutil\n6\tfrom pathlib import Path\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom ..perception.capture import Frame, grab_camera, grab_screen\n10\tfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, host_id, probe_always_on, probe_gpu_vlm\n11\t\n12\t_SCREEN_TOOLS = (\"scrot\", \"gnome-screenshot\", \"spectacle\", \"import\")\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tclass LinuxBackend:\n16\t    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n17\t        return grab_screen()\n18\t\n19\t    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n20\t        return grab_camera()\n21\t\n22\t    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor:\n23\t        return CapabilityDescriptor(\n24\t            host_id=host_id(), os=\"linux\",\n25\t            has_screen=any(shutil.which(t) for t in _SCREEN_TOOLS),\n26\t            has_camera=Path(\"/dev/video0\").exists(),\n27\t            has_gpu_vlm=probe_gpu_vlm(), always_on=probe_always_on())\n28\t"}]},"uuid":"a28793b3-8ccd-4632-aaa9-07e368e5d4ca","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:49.380Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4abb3769-8d8f-421c-aadd-d6ce807882a0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"a28793b3-8ccd-4632-aaa9-07e368e5d4ca","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA5sgFV33xuH1YAgnyJU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NJmAZvaE5rbtwmSdJAqUc9","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/platform/macos.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3028,"cache_read_input_tokens":32858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3028,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":36,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA5scxnmcvBttmVuatmy","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"8d6dee8a-9dc2-418a-8f58-6b322bf2d347","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:49.843Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"8d6dee8a-9dc2-418a-8f58-6b322bf2d347","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NJmAZvaE5rbtwmSdJAqUc9","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"macOS backend (Phase 7, WS-D) — screen via `screencapture`; camera left as an honest gap\n2\t(`imagesnap` is an optional add). Additive; returns None when the native tool is absent.\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport shutil\n6\timport subprocess\n7\timport tempfile\n8\tfrom pathlib import Path\n9\tfrom typing import Optional\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..perception.capture import Frame, _ocr\n12\tfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, host_id, probe_always_on, probe_gpu_vlm\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tclass MacOSBackend:\n16\t    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n17\t        if not shutil.which(\"screencapture\"):\n18\t            return None\n19\t        out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-screen-\")\n20\t        try:\n21\t            subprocess.run([\"screencapture\", \"-x\", out], capture_output=True, timeout=20)\n22\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n23\t            return None\n24\t        if Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n25\t            return Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n26\t        return None\n27\t\n28\t    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n29\t        return None                                   # honest gap (optional imagesnap add)\n30\t\n31\t    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor:\n32\t        return CapabilityDescriptor(\n33\t            host_id=host_id(), os=\"macos\", has_screen=bool(shutil.which(\"screencapture\")),\n34\t            has_camera=False, has_gpu_vlm=probe_gpu_vlm(), always_on=probe_always_on())\n35\t"}]},"uuid":"93072afe-6e0d-419a-8829-643a814ab7af","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:49.862Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8d6dee8a-9dc2-418a-8f58-6b322bf2d347","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6d5bd130-63ec-4712-bdd6-b4d477e3ccfc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Un5uzKPdPMpJiqLfXa8Kwq","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Windows backend (Phase 7, WS-D) — screen via the optional `mss` package; camera an honest gap.\n2\tAdditive; returns None when `mss` is unavailable.\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport tempfile\n6\tfrom pathlib import Path\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom ..perception.capture import Frame, _ocr\n10\tfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, host_id, probe_always_on, probe_gpu_vlm\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tdef _has_mss() -> bool:\n14\t    try:\n15\t        import mss  # noqa: F401\n16\t        return True\n17\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n18\t        return False\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass WindowsBackend:\n22\t    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n23\t        try:\n24\t            import mss\n25\t            out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-screen-\")\n26\t            with mss.mss() as sct:\n27\t                sct.shot(output=out)\n28\t            if Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n29\t                return Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n30\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no mss / grab failure → honest None\n31\t            return None\n32\t        return None\n33\t\n34\t    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n35\t        return None\n36\t\n37\t    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor:\n38\t        return CapabilityDescriptor(\n39\t            host_id=host_id(), os=\"windows\", has_screen=_has_mss(), has_camera=False,\n40\t            has_gpu_vlm=probe_gpu_vlm(), always_on=probe_always_on())\n41\t"}]},"uuid":"3686141d-1b6f-47cb-9b9f-a1840e85592e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:50.381Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6d5bd130-63ec-4712-bdd6-b4d477e3ccfc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"3686141d-1b6f-47cb-9b9f-a1840e85592e","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA5sgFV33xuH1YAgnyJU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JmK6c25W4mnUjRfiUTtXks","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/platform/secrets.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3028,"cache_read_input_tokens":32858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3028,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":36,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA5scxnmcvBttmVuatmy","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"9ded3ba5-151a-4e85-a0e0-11b1b9e39d8d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:50.839Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"9ded3ba5-151a-4e85-a0e0-11b1b9e39d8d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JmK6c25W4mnUjRfiUTtXks","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SecretStore (Phase 7, WS-D D-ii) — keyring-first secret handling. Reads/writes API keys via the\n2\tOS keyring (macOS Keychain / Windows Credential Manager / libsecret) when the `keyring` package is\n3\tpresent; otherwise falls back to the plaintext `~/.sigil/sigil.env` that `config._load_env_file`\n4\talready loads. Secrets NEVER enter the append-only spine, a log, or a network payload — this is\n5\towner-local plumbing that keeps them off the plaintext file and off the wire (WS-D transport carries\n6\tonly `{seq,tier,kind}`).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport os\n10\tfrom typing import Optional\n11\t\n12\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n13\t\n14\t_SERVICE = \"sigil\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tclass SecretStore:\n18\t    def __init__(self):\n19\t        try:\n20\t            import keyring\n21\t            self._kr = keyring\n22\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no keyring backend → env fallback only\n23\t            self._kr = None\n24\t\n25\t    @property\n26\t    def backend(self) -> str:\n27\t        return \"keyring\" if self._kr is not None else \"envfile\"\n28\t\n29\t    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[str]:\n30\t        if self._kr is not None:\n31\t            try:\n32\t                v = self._kr.get_password(_SERVICE, key)\n33\t                if v is not None:\n34\t                    return v\n35\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n36\t                pass\n37\t        return os.environ.get(key)                    # sigil.env is loaded into env at config import\n38\t\n39\t    def set(self, key: str, value: str) -> str:\n40\t        \"\"\"Store a secret. Returns the backend used. Prefers the keyring; falls back to sigil.env\n41\t        (0600). Never writes the value anywhere else.\"\"\"\n42\t        if self._kr is not None:\n43\t            try:\n44\t                self._kr.set_password(_SERVICE, key, value)\n45\t                os.environ[key] = value               # make it live this process too\n46\t                return \"keyring\"\n47\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n48\t                pass\n49\t        self._env_upsert(key, value)\n50\t        os.environ[key] = value\n51\t        return \"envfile\"\n52\t\n53\t    @staticmethod\n54\t    def _env_upsert(key: str, value: str) -> None:\n55\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n56\t        SIGIL_HOME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n57\t        lines = []\n58\t        found = False\n59\t        try:\n60\t            for ln in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n61\t                if ln.split(\"=\", 1)[0].strip() == key:\n62\t                    lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\"); found = True\n63\t                else:\n64\t                    lines.append(ln)\n65\t        except OSError:\n66\t            pass\n67\t        if not found:\n68\t            lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\")\n69\t        f.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n70\t        try:\n71\t            os.chmod(f, 0o600)\n72\t        except OSError:\n73\t            pass\n74\t"}]},"uuid":"4ebf3ac6-74c8-49d2-8a2c-0563595fe20b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:50.882Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9ded3ba5-151a-4e85-a0e0-11b1b9e39d8d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2e6bc5f7-3734-46fa-a1f9-10a6c311cc17","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017ZaNxs7WRXHeGKsv8Tcuiz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-OS backend abstraction (Phase 7, WS-D D-i). The stack above (perception, voice) speaks the\n2\t`Frame` contract; this layer selects a `HostBackend` for the current OS at runtime, so the same\n3\tcode runs on Linux/macOS/Windows. Linux wraps the existing capture verbatim; macOS/Windows are\n4\tadditive; every backend returns `None` on unavailability (the honest-gap posture). Offense-free.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\timport sys\n8\t\n9\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n10\tfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, HostBackend\n11\t\n12\tassert_no_offense()\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef host() -> HostBackend:\n16\t    \"\"\"The backend for this OS (selected by `sys.platform`).\"\"\"\n17\t    if sys.platform == \"darwin\":\n18\t        from .macos import MacOSBackend\n19\t        return MacOSBackend()\n20\t    if sys.platform.startswith(\"win\"):\n21\t        from .windows import WindowsBackend\n22\t        return WindowsBackend()\n23\t    from .linux import LinuxBackend\n24\t    return LinuxBackend()\n25\t\n26\t\n27\t__all__ = [\"host\", \"HostBackend\", \"CapabilityDescriptor\"]\n28\t"}]},"uuid":"38757d26-d45f-41fb-98d1-79a4d806505e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:51.311Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2e6bc5f7-3734-46fa-a1f9-10a6c311cc17","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2112c6e9-7f36-4577-aa27-891dd02be544","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HE1S2r6PmULuJhonGKA3XR","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Shared authentication for governance events (Phase 6). Every state-changing governance event\n2\t(promotion grant/revoke, kill engage/release, approval approve/deny) carries an Ed25519 signature by\n3\tthe OWNER key over its canonical authenticated CORE fields, plus the signer's pubkey. Verification is\n4\tfail-closed: no trusted key, no signature, a pubkey that isn't the trusted owner key, or a tampered\n5\tcore → NOT authentic. This is the primitive that makes \"zero unauthorized A2/A3\" provable from the\n6\tlog rather than promised (a forged governance event never verifies, so it is ignored).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom typing import Optional, Sequence\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sign, verify_one\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef _canon(core: dict) -> bytes:\n15\t    m = canonical_json(core)\n16\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef signed_payload(core: dict, owner_key) -> dict:\n20\t    \"\"\"core = the authenticated fields (must be JSON-canonicalizable). Returns core + {sig, pubkey}.\n21\t    With no owner key, sig/pubkey are None → the event will never verify (a caller that requires\n22\t    authentication must reject it).\"\"\"\n23\t    sig = sign(owner_key.private_key_b64, _canon(core)) if owner_key else None\n24\t    return {**core, \"sig\": sig, \"pubkey\": owner_key.public_key_b64 if owner_key else None}\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef verify_signed(payload: dict, core_fields: Sequence[str], trusted_pubkey: Optional[str]) -> bool:\n28\t    \"\"\"True iff `payload` carries a valid owner signature over its declared core fields. Fail-closed.\"\"\"\n29\t    if not trusted_pubkey:\n30\t        return False\n31\t    sig = payload.get(\"sig\")\n32\t    if not sig or payload.get(\"pubkey\") != trusted_pubkey:\n33\t        return False\n34\t    core = {k: payload.get(k) for k in core_fields}\n35\t    return verify_one(trusted_pubkey, _canon(core), sig)\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"d60943f4-b7f6-4fb9-9bbb-0e0d48dea431","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:56.819Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2112c6e9-7f36-4577-aa27-891dd02be544","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"5b682436-b4cf-43bd-805a-5c6228d5867b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015BppmaFQG4oVaQ9eYn6XSY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Owner identity for governance authentication (Phase 6). The trust anchor is the SAME solo-owner\n2\tEd25519 keypair the spine checkpoint already persists (`KEYS_DIR/owner.priv|pub`, the 1-of-1 trust\n3\troot) — governance events (promotion grants, kill release, approvals) are signed by it and verified\n4\tagainst it. Reads are fail-closed: a missing key returns None, so nothing forged is ever trusted.\n5\t\n6\t`owner_pubkey()`/`owner_keypair()` READ the anchor (never generate — verification must not mint\n7\ttrust). `ensure_owner_keypair()` is for owner-initiated SIGNING actions (CLI engage/grant/approve):\n8\tit generates+persists the anchor once if absent, mirroring the checkpoint's `_owner_keys()`.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport os\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom ..config import KEYS_DIR\n15\tfrom ..reuse import KeyPair, generate_keypair\n16\t\n17\t_PRIV = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.priv\"\n18\t_PUB = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.pub\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef owner_pubkey() -> Optional[str]:\n22\t    try:\n23\t        return (_PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip() or None)\n24\t    except OSError:\n25\t        return None\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef owner_keypair() -> Optional[KeyPair]:\n29\t    try:\n30\t        priv = _PRIV.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n31\t        pub = _PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n32\t    except OSError:\n33\t        return None\n34\t    return KeyPair(public_key_b64=pub, private_key_b64=priv) if priv and pub else None\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef ensure_owner_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n38\t    \"\"\"Return the persisted owner keypair, generating+persisting it once if absent (owner signing\n39\t    path only). Same key material the checkpoint uses, so governance and the spine head share one\n40\t    owner identity.\"\"\"\n41\t    kp = owner_keypair()\n42\t    if kp is not None:\n43\t        return kp\n44\t    KEYS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n45\t    kp = generate_keypair()\n46\t    _PRIV.write_text(kp.private_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n47\t    _PUB.write_text(kp.public_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n48\t    for f in (_PRIV,):\n49\t        try:\n50\t            os.chmod(f, 0o600)\n51\t        except OSError:\n52\t            pass\n53\t    return kp\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"9a1d9971-1f8d-4e17-8140-0f4f83819a2e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:35:57.329Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5b682436-b4cf-43bd-805a-5c6228d5867b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

SIGIL\n4\timports NO `framework.*` module at all, which is the strongest form of the sovereignty\n5\tdoctrine (§1.3 / §12): the offensive engine cannot even be loaded into a SIGIL process.\n6\t`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport sys\n11\t\n12\tfrom .canonical import canonical_json, digest_payload, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n13\tfrom .chain import append_entry, build_chain, sign_head, verify_chain, verify_head\n14\tfrom .crypto import generate_keypair, sign, verify_one, verify_threshold, KeyPair\n15\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n16\t\n17\t__all__ = [\n18\t    \"canonical_json\", \"digest_payload\", \"evidence_signing_bytes\", \"sha256_hex\",\n19\t    \"append_entry\", \"build_chain\", \"sign_head\", \"verify_chain\", \"verify_head\",\n20\t    \"generate_keypair\", \"sign\", \"verify_one\", \"verify_threshold\", \"KeyPair\",\n21\t    \"AuthorizerKey\", \"ChainEntry\", \"Signature\", \"SignedChainHead\", \"TrustRoot\",\n22\t    \"assert_no_offense\",\n23\t]\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef assert_no_offense() -> None:\n27\t    \"\"\"Fail-closed: NO framework.* module may be loaded in a SIGIL process (doctrine §12).\"\"\"\n28\t    leaked = [m for m in sys.modules if m == \"framework\" or m.startswith(\"framework.\")]\n29\t    if leaked:\n30\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"SIGIL sovereignty violation: engine modules loaded: {leaked}\")\n31\t"}]},"uuid":"1b847150-2fe0-4866-9aa4-3ee4329f11d5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:36:09.580Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0b37fd89-e232-441b-85a9-6430e4e651c6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"4feec458-ba05-4c48-a0c0-1b4aaf3e8033","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013dBWTZ24tpa9rWsRshaTPT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Ed25519 primitives + m-of-n threshold verify.\n2\t\n3\tVENDORED VERBATIM from CRUCIBLE `framework/v2/entitlement/crypto.py` (owner's own work).\n4\t\"We do not roll our own crypto\" — Ed25519 is pyca `cryptography`. Signing helpers are\n5\tprovisioning-only; the runtime only ever verifies. The sole change from the source is a\n6\tlocal `IntegrityError` in place of the framework's `EntitlementError` (decoupling).\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport base64\n11\timport binascii\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\t\n14\tfrom cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature\n15\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import (\n16\t    Ed25519PrivateKey,\n17\t    Ed25519PublicKey,\n18\t)\n19\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.serialization import (\n20\t    Encoding,\n21\t    NoEncryption,\n22\t    PrivateFormat,\n23\t    PublicFormat,\n24\t)\n25\t\n26\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, Signature, TrustRoot\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tclass IntegrityError(Exception):\n30\t    \"\"\"Malformed key/signature material (a provisioning error, not an attacker path).\"\"\"\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _b64decode_exact(value: str, expected_len: int, what: str) -> bytes:\n34\t    try:\n35\t        raw = base64.b64decode(value, validate=True)\n36\t    except (ValueError, binascii.Error) as e:\n37\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} is not valid base64: {e}\") from e\n38\t    if len(raw) != expected_len:\n39\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} decodes to {len(raw)} bytes, expected {expected_len}\")\n40\t    return raw\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef load_public_key(public_key_b64: str) -> Ed25519PublicKey:\n44\t    return Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(_b64decode_exact(public_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 public key\"))\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef verify_one(public_key_b64: str, message: bytes, signature_b64: str) -> bool:\n48\t    \"\"\"True iff a valid Ed25519 signature; False on bad sig; raises only on malformed material.\"\"\"\n49\t    pub = load_public_key(public_key_b64)\n50\t    sig = _b64decode_exact(signature_b64, 64, \"Ed25519 signature\")\n51\t    try:\n52\t        pub.verify(sig, message)\n53\t        return True\n54\t    except InvalidSignature:\n55\t        return False\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n59\tclass ThresholdResult:\n60\t    satisfied: bool\n61\t    valid_signers: tuple[str, ...]\n62\t    threshold: int\n63\t    reason: str\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef verify_threshold(message: bytes, signatures: list[Signature], trust_root: TrustRoot) -> ThresholdResult:\n67\t    \"\"\"Count distinct trust-root authorisers with a valid signature; compare to threshold.\"\"\"\n68\t    by_id: dict[str, AuthorizerKey] = {a.key_id: a for a in trust_root.authorizers}\n69\t    valid: list[str] = []\n70\t    seen: set[str] = set()\n71\t    for sig in signatures:\n72\t        if sig.key_id in seen:\n73\t            continue\n74\t        seen.add(sig.key_id)\n75\t        authorizer = by_id.get(sig.key_id)\n76\t        if authorizer is None:\n77\t            continue\n78\t        if verify_one(authorizer.public_key_b64, message, sig.signature_b64):\n79\t            valid.append(sig.key_id)\n80\t    satisfied = len(valid) >= trust_root.threshold\n81\t    reason = (\n82\t        f\"{len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) >= threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n83\t        if satisfied else\n84\t        f\"only {len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) < threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n85\t    )\n86\t    return ThresholdResult(satisfied=satisfied, valid_signers=tuple(valid), threshold=trust_root.threshold, reason=reason)\n87\t\n88\t\n89\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n90\tclass KeyPair:\n91\t    public_key_b64: str\n92\t    private_key_b64: str\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tdef generate_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n96\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()\n97\t    raw_priv = priv.private_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PrivateFormat.Raw, NoEncryption())\n98\t    raw_pub = priv.public_key().public_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PublicFormat.Raw)\n99\t    return KeyPair(\n100\t        public_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_pub).decode(\"ascii\"),\n101\t        private_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_priv).decode(\"ascii\"),\n102\t    )\n103\t\n104\t\n105\tdef sign(private_key_b64: str, message: bytes) -> str:\n106\t    raw = _b64decode_exact(private_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 private key\")\n107\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(raw)\n108\t    return base64.b64encode(priv.sign(message)).decode(\"ascii\")\n109\t"}]},"uuid":"5549221b-3008-411f-b853-c05d31d0324f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:36:10.062Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4feec458-ba05-4c48-a0c0-1b4aaf3e8033","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"0c4cd563-3ec5-4046-893d-3d32776a95ad","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Jhuer3i2siWbuou8LJvamM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Deterministic canonical bytes + digests for spine integrity.\n2\t\n3\tVENDORED VERBATIM from CRUCIBLE `framework/v2/evidence/canonical.py` (owner's own work).\n4\tCopied — not imported — so SIGIL owns its integrity primitives, fully decoupled from the\n5\toffensive engine's environment (sovereignty doctrine). Behaviour is byte-identical.\n6\t\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport hashlib\n10\timport json\n11\tfrom typing import Any, Final\n12\t\n13\t# Never change without a schema bump + migration (invalidates all existing signatures).\n14\t_EVIDENCE_DOMAIN: Final[bytes] = b\"crucible-evidence-v1\\x00\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef canonical_json(payload: Any) -> bytes:\n18\t    \"\"\"Deterministic UTF-8 JSON: sorted keys (recursive), compact separators.\"\"\"\n19\t    return json.dumps(\n20\t        payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), ensure_ascii=False,\n21\t    ).encode(\"utf-8\")\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tdef sha256_hex(data: bytes) -> str:\n25\t    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef digest_payload(payload: Any) -> str:\n29\t    \"\"\"sha256 of a JSON-serialisable payload in canonical form.\"\"\"\n30\t    return sha256_hex(canonical_json(payload))\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef evidence_signing_bytes(certificate_payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:\n34\t    \"\"\"The exact bytes signed/verified (canonical JSON under the domain tag).\"\"\"\n35\t    return _EVIDENCE_DOMAIN + canonical_json(certificate_payload)\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"f736d73a-1680-4f2f-acea-aa0b56ccbba5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:36:10.258Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0c4cd563-3ec5-4046-893d-3d32776a95ad","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
