{"parentUuid":"22560dfb-b064-4c78-b521-7fbfb23ad973","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GtHTAwdicmXupfN5YrS7id","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only, hash-chained JSONL spine (SIGIL §6.1, D1).\n2\t\n3\tReuses CRUCIBLE's tamper-evident chain verbatim (`sigil.reuse`): each line carries\n4\t`{seq, prev_hash, entry_hash}` where `entry_hash` links prev+cert_digest+seq. The\n5\t`cert_digest` is over the record's CONTENT only (scope/kind/source/actor/payload/\n6\tparent/supersedes) — NOT the wallclock `ts` — so the chain is replay-stable. Appends\n7\tare O(1): read the last line's entry, `append_entry`, write.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport os\n13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n14\tfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..config import SCOPE, SPINE_PATH\n17\tfrom ..reuse import ChainEntry, append_entry, build_chain, digest_payload, verify_chain\n18\tfrom .models import SpineRecord, now_iso\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _last_nonempty_line(path: Path) -> str | None:\n22\t    \"\"\"Read the last non-empty line without loading the file (seek-from-end).\"\"\"\n23\t    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n24\t        f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n25\t        end = f.tell()\n26\t        if end == 0:\n27\t            return None\n28\t        buf = b\"\"\n29\t        pos = end\n30\t        while pos > 0:\n31\t            step = min(8192, pos)\n32\t            pos -= step\n33\t            f.seek(pos)\n34\t            buf = f.read(step) + buf\n35\t            lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n36\t            if lines and (pos == 0 or buf.count(b\"\\n\") >= 2):\n37\t                return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\")\n38\t        lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n39\t        return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\") if lines else None\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tclass SpineStore:\n43\t    def __init__(self, path: Path | str = SPINE_PATH) -> None:\n44\t        self.path = Path(path)\n45\t        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        self._last: ChainEntry | None = self._read_last_entry()\n47\t\n48\t    # --- write --------------------------------------------------------------------\n49\t    def append(\n50\t        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n51\t        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n52\t        ts: str | None = None,\n53\t    ) -> int:\n54\t        content = {\n55\t            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n56\t            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n57\t        }\n58\t        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n59\t        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n60\t        record = {\n61\t            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n62\t            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n63\t        }\n64\t        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n65\t            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n66\t        self._last = entry\n67\t        return entry.seq\n68\t\n69\t    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n70\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n71\t        if not self.path.exists():\n72\t            return\n73\t        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n74\t            for line in f:\n75\t                line = line.strip()\n76\t                if not line:\n77\t                    continue\n78\t                d = json.loads(line)\n79\t                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n80\t                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n81\t\n82\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n83\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n84\t            if r.seq == seq:\n85\t                return r\n86\t            if r.seq > seq:\n87\t                break\n88\t        return None\n89\t\n90\t    @property\n91\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n92\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n93\t\n94\t    def count(self) -> int:\n95\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n96\t\n97\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n98\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n99\t        return [\n100\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n101\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n102\t        ]\n103\t\n104\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n105\t        \"\"\"Two-layer UNKEYED integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n106\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link cleanly\n107\t        (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). This proves internal CONSISTENCY, not authenticity:\n108\t        a naive payload edit fails (1), and a mid-chain digest edit cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash\n109\t        break caught by (2) — BUT a writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash for the tip (no\n110\t        successor to cascade into) or forward-cascades a fork produces a self-consistent chain that\n111\t        passes here. Resistance to a recompute-capable writer is the owner-SIGNED head's job\n112\t        (`checkpoint.verify_checkpoint`, Ed25519 + monotonic last_seq). Use this for corruption/\n113\t        naive-tamper detection; use the signed head for tamper-EVIDENCE.\"\"\"\n114\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n115\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n116\t            content = {\n117\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n118\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n119\t            }\n120\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n121\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n122\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n123\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n124\t\n125\t    def _read_last_entry(self) -> ChainEntry | None:\n126\t        line = _last_nonempty_line(self.path) if self.path.exists() else None\n127\t        if not line:\n128\t            return None\n129\t        d = json.loads(line)\n130\t        return ChainEntry(seq=d[\"seq\"], prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"])\n131\t"}]},"uuid":"78be80dd-7dc0-4b11-a235-4482ff4c4862","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:36:59.455Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"22560dfb-b064-4c78-b521-7fbfb23ad973","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"78be80dd-7dc0-4b11-a235-4482ff4c4862","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA5uc9ERKC5xAWX94MYg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Cuc3QkR1M4xCCJgXM6iUci","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/tail.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3469,"cache_read_input_tokens":43328,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3469,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA5uaprfPUvrC2bcqraB","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"468286da-73ca-4455-821e-fe637373d994","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:36:59.949Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"468286da-73ca-4455-821e-fe637373d994","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Cuc3QkR1M4xCCJgXM6iUci","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SpineTailer (Phase 7, P0.2) — a live tail over the append-only, poll-only spine with an HONEST,\n2\ttwo-layer integrity model (red-pen-hardened: unkeyed hashes alone cannot resist a writer who can\n3\trecompute them):\n4\t\n5\t  • `integrity_ok` (CHEAP, per-poll, unkeyed) — per-record binding+derivation (`verify_record`) plus\n6\t    cross-record LINKAGE (this record's `prev_hash` == the last emitted `entry_hash`). This catches\n7\t    ACCIDENTAL corruption, a naive stale-field tamper, a deletion/reorder/gap. It does NOT resist a\n8\t    file-writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash (a tip tamper or a forward-cascaded fork stays\n9\t    internally consistent) — so it is necessary but not sufficient, and is never presented as proof\n10\t    of authenticity on its own.\n11\t\n12\t  • `anchored` (KEYED, via `check_anchor`) — the owner-SIGNED head (`checkpoint.classify_head` /\n13\t    `verify_head`, Ed25519 1-of-1 trust root, monotonic `last_seq`) notarizes the prefix\n14\t    [0 .. signed_last_seq]. A recompute-fork or truncation AT OR BELOW the signed head is\n15\t    unforgeable without the owner key and is caught here; a monotonic high-water catches a rollback\n16\t    of even the un-notarized tail. Records with seq > signed_last_seq are the FRESH, not-yet-keyed\n17\t    tail — emitted `anchored=False` (well-formed but not tamper-proof), keyed at the next `sigil sign`.\n18\t\n19\tA consumer (UI/mobile) shows `integrity_ok=False` as \"integrity broken\", and treats only\n20\t`anchored=True` records as owner-notarized truth; the un-notarized tail is labelled as such, never\n21\tdressed up as proven. One primitive, two consumers — the UI SSE feed and the mobile push notifier —\n22\tboth drive `poll()` on a tick and fan events out via `Broadcaster`.\"\"\"\n23\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n24\t\n25\tfrom collections import deque\n26\tfrom typing import Optional\n27\t\n28\tfrom ..config import HEAD_PATH\n29\tfrom ..reuse import SignedChainHead\n30\tfrom ..reuse.chain import _GENESIS_PREV\n31\tfrom .checkpoint import classify_head, trust_root\n32\tfrom .store import SpineStore\n33\tfrom .verify import verify_record\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef _shape(record, *, integrity_ok: bool, integrity_reason: str, anchored: bool) -> dict:\n37\t    \"\"\"The cited atom shape emitted to consumers. `integrity_ok` = unkeyed well-formedness;\n38\t    `anchored` = covered by the owner-signed head (keyed). `text` is best-effort, never fabricated.\"\"\"\n39\t    return {\n40\t        \"seq\": record.seq, \"kind\": record.kind, \"source\": record.source, \"actor\": record.actor,\n41\t        \"ts\": record.ts, \"entry_hash\": record.entry_hash, \"text\": record.text(),\n42\t        \"integrity_ok\": integrity_ok, \"integrity_reason\": integrity_reason, \"anchored\": anchored,\n43\t    }\n44\t\n45\t\n46\tclass SpineTailer:\n47\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, since_seq: int = -1,\n48\t                 signed_head: Optional[SignedChainHead] = None, tr=None):\n49\t        \"\"\"Emit records with seq > `since_seq`. `signed_head`/`tr` may be injected (tests); otherwise\n50\t        the owner-signed head is loaded from `HEAD_PATH` + the persisted trust root.\"\"\"\n51\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n52\t        self.cursor = since_seq\n53\t        if since_seq < 0:\n54\t            self._last_hash = _GENESIS_PREV\n55\t        else:\n56\t            anchor = self.store.get(since_seq)\n57\t            self._last_hash = anchor.entry_hash if anchor else _GENESIS_PREV\n58\t        self._injected_head = signed_head\n59\t        self._injected_tr = tr\n60\t        self._high_water = -1                           # monotonic anti-rollback water mark\n61\t        self._signed_last_seq = -1\n62\t        self.check_anchor()                             # establish the initial keyed anchor (reads disk)\n63\t\n64\t    def poll(self) -> list[dict]:\n65\t        \"\"\"Newly-appended records (seq > cursor), each with the cheap `integrity_ok` verdict and the\n66\t        `anchored` (keyed) flag from the last `check_anchor`. On an integrity failure the event is\n67\t        emitted flagged (not dropped); the cursor still advances so the feed never re-emits or stalls.\"\"\"\n68\t        out: list[dict] = []\n69\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=self.cursor):\n70\t            atom_ok, reason = verify_record(r)\n71\t            if r.prev_hash != self._last_hash:\n72\t                ok = False\n73\t                reason = f\"linkage break at seq {r.seq}: prev_hash != last emitted entry_hash (rewind/fork/gap)\"\n74\t            else:\n75\t                ok = atom_ok\n76\t            out.append(_shape(r, integrity_ok=ok, integrity_reason=reason,\n77\t                              anchored=(r.seq <= self._signed_last_seq)))\n78\t            self.cursor = r.seq\n79\t            self._last_hash = r.entry_hash\n80\t        return out\n81\t\n82\t    def check_anchor(self) -> dict:\n83\t        \"\"\"The KEYED verdict. Verify the owner-signed head over the current chain (unforgeable without\n84\t        the owner key): a recompute-fork or truncation at/below the signed head → `anchor_ok=False`;\n85\t        a monotonic high-water rollback of even the un-notarized tail → `rollback` set. Updates\n86\t        `signed_last_seq` so `poll()` can flag records beyond it as the un-notarized tail.\"\"\"\n87\t        entries = self.store.entries()                  # FRESH read from disk (not the cached head)\n88\t        head_seq = entries[-1].seq if entries else -1\n89\t        rollback = None\n90\t        if head_seq < self._high_water:\n91\t            rollback = f\"ROLLBACK: on-disk head seq {head_seq} < monotonic high-water {self._high_water}\"\n92\t        else:\n93\t            self._high_water = head_seq\n94\t        head, tr = self._resolve_head()\n95\t        if head is None:\n96\t            self._signed_last_seq = -1\n97\t            return {\"anchor_ok\": False, \"signed_last_seq\": -1, \"rollback\": rollback,\n98\t                    \"reason\": \"no signed checkpoint — the live tail is well-formed but UN-NOTARIZED \"\n99\t                              \"(run `sigil sign` to anchor)\"}\n100\t        try:\n101\t            ok, reason = classify_head(head, entries, tr)\n102\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a malformed head must FAIL closed, never crash the feed\n103\t            self._signed_last_seq = -1\n104\t            return {\"anchor_ok\": False, \"signed_last_seq\": -1, \"rollback\": rollback,\n105\t                    \"reason\": f\"anchor unverifiable ({e})\"}\n106\t        self._signed_last_seq = head.last_seq if ok else -1\n107\t        return {\"anchor_ok\": ok and rollback is None, \"signed_last_seq\": self._signed_last_seq,\n108\t                \"rollback\": rollback, \"reason\": reason}\n109\t\n110\t    def _resolve_head(self):\n111\t        if self._injected_head is not None:\n112\t            return self._injected_head, self._injected_tr\n113\t        try:\n114\t            if not HEAD_PATH.exists():\n115\t                return None, None\n116\t            head = SignedChainHead.model_validate_json(HEAD_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n117\t            return head, trust_root()\n118\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no/broken head → unkeyed live tail (honest, fail-closed)\n119\t            return None, None\n120\t\n121\t\n122\tclass Broadcaster:\n123\t    \"\"\"Fan-out of tail events to N subscribers with bounded per-subscriber queues. On overflow the\n124\t    oldest events are dropped and the subscriber is marked `lagged` so it resyncs from the cursor\n125\t    (never a fabricated 'you saw everything').\"\"\"\n126\t    def __init__(self, maxlen: int = 512):\n127\t        self.maxlen = maxlen\n128\t        self._subs: dict[int, deque] = {}\n129\t        self._lagged: dict[int, bool] = {}\n130\t        self._next_id = 0\n131\t\n132\t    def subscribe(self) -> int:\n133\t        sid = self._next_id\n134\t        self._next_id += 1\n135\t        self._subs[sid] = deque(maxlen=self.maxlen)\n136\t        self._lagged[sid] = False\n137\t        return sid\n138\t\n139\t    def unsubscribe(self, sid: int) -> None:\n140\t        self._subs.pop(sid, None)\n141\t        self._lagged.pop(sid, None)\n142\t\n143\t    def publish(self, events: list[dict]) -> None:\n144\t        for sid, q in self._subs.items():\n145\t            for ev in events:\n146\t                if len(q) == self.maxlen:        # would drop the oldest → the subscriber lagged\n147\t                    self._lagged[sid] = True\n148\t                q.append(ev)\n149\t\n150\t    def drain(self, sid: int) -> tuple[list[dict], bool]:\n151\t        \"\"\"Return (events, lagged) and clear both. `lagged=True` tells the consumer to resync.\"\"\"\n152\t        q = self._subs.get(sid)\n153\t        if q is None:\n154\t            return [], False\n155\t        events = list(q)\n156\t        q.clear()\n157\t        lagged = self._lagged.get(sid, False)\n158\t        self._lagged[sid] = False\n159\t        return events, lagged\n160\t"}]},"uuid":"6b52a17d-dad8-4cad-a38f-1a5362e768d4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:36:59.970Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"468286da-73ca-4455-821e-fe637373d994","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ae95d897-f801-4f8c-aa15-1a2d39b64a09","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019tw5xpZXVvwiUhg83chAME","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Voice → KERNEL dispatch. Recognized text is routed through the Phase-1 KERNEL (`sigil-kernel\n2\task`), so a voice request crosses the SAME T0 router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as any\n3\tother — voice is just another interface onto the one authorized path. The spoken response is the\n4\tKERNEL's answer body (the bracketed [T0…]/[WARDEN…] status lines are stripped).\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\timport subprocess\n8\tfrom pathlib import Path\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _default_bin() -> str:\n12\t    \"\"\"Resolve the kernel binary portably (WS-D D7): an explicit env override, then package-relative\n13\t    (the repo the package lives in), then the legacy dev path, then PATH.\"\"\"\n14\t    import os\n15\t    exe = \"sigil-kernel.exe\" if os.name == \"nt\" else \"sigil-kernel\"\n16\t    env = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_KERNEL_BIN\")\n17\t    if env and Path(env).exists():\n18\t        return env\n19\t    # sigil/voice/dispatch.py → repo root is three parents up (sigil/<pkg>/voice/..)\n20\t    repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n21\t    for base in (repo_root, Path(\"/home/kali/sigil\")):\n22\t        for p in (f\"kernel/target/release/{exe}\", f\"kernel/target/debug/{exe}\"):\n23\t            if (base / p).exists():\n24\t                return str(base / p)\n25\t    return exe\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tclass KernelDispatch:\n29\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 60):\n30\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n31\t        self.timeout = timeout\n32\t\n33\t    # exact KERNEL status-line prefixes to strip (so a legitimate answer line that merely starts\n34\t    # with '[' is NOT dropped, and a BLOCKED/error is NOT masked as an answer).\n35\t    _STATUS = (\"[t0\", \"[warden\", \"[blocked\", \"[direct\", \"[dispatch\")\n36\t\n37\t    def send(self, text: str) -> str:\n38\t        text = (text or \"\").strip()\n39\t        if not text:\n40\t            return \"I didn't catch that.\"\n41\t        try:\n42\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"ask\", text],\n43\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n44\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:\n45\t            return f\"the kernel is unavailable ({e})\"\n46\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n47\t            tail = (proc.stderr or proc.stdout).strip().splitlines()\n48\t            return f\"that request was refused or errored: {tail[-1][:200]}\" if tail else \"that request was refused.\"\n49\t        if any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(\"[blocked\") for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()):\n50\t            return \"that action needs your approval — it wasn't run.\"\n51\t        body = [ln.strip() for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()\n52\t                if ln.strip() and not any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(s) for s in self._STATUS)]\n53\t        answer = \" \".join(body).strip()\n54\t        return answer[:600] if answer else \"there's no answer for that in memory yet.\"\n55\t"}]},"uuid":"d93c6e2d-f87d-4d30-a412-48656a1c99dd","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:37:00.170Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ae95d897-f801-4f8c-aa15-1a2d39b64a09","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"fb994616-4342-40e3-bfc4-b949b030cd70","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01N1h6jraaBnH8xqfrCLTFMW","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\n2\tmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n3\t\n4\tASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\n5\tengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\n6\tdangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\n7\tagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\n8\tso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom typing import Optional\n12\t\n13\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n14\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n15\t\n16\tSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n17\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass KillSwitch:\n21\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n22\t        self.store = store\n23\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n24\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n25\t\n26\t    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n27\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n28\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n29\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n30\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n31\t\n32\t    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n33\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n34\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n35\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n39\t        engaged = False\n40\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n41\t            p = r.payload\n42\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n43\t                continue\n44\t            state = p.get(\"state\")\n45\t            if state == \"engaged\":\n46\t                engaged = True                      # honor ANY engage — halting is fail-safe\n47\t            elif state == \"released\" and verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n48\t                engaged = False                     # only an OWNER-SIGNED release un-halts (fail-closed)\n49\t        return engaged\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"211f725e-f87e-4843-b0f7-4313e86a6989","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:37:08.064Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fb994616-4342-40e3-bfc4-b949b030cd70","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"1097b038-5a98-4b86-9a28-12e9f546314a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014bTrNDmH2FXkPszp7nzQQ3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The episodic spine record — one immutable event, source of truth (SIGIL §6.1).\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n5\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n6\tfrom typing import Any\n7\t\n8\t# event kinds (superset of the CRUCIBLE blackboard EventKind, extended for SIGIL sources)\n9\tKINDS = frozenset({\n10\t    \"message\", \"tool_call\", \"tool_result\", \"decision\", \"commitment\",\n11\t    \"commit\", \"document\", \"brief\", \"refusal\", \"email\", \"session\",\n12\t    \"entity\", \"contradiction\",  # promoted by the ARCHIVIST consolidation pass (§6.3)\n13\t    \"warden_checkpoint\",        # WARDEN (Phase 1) cross-anchors its action-log head here (anti-rollback)\n14\t    \"event\", \"finding\", \"interaction\", \"draft\",  # the agent mesh (Phase 3, §4): SENTINEL/BASTION/ENVOY\n15\t    \"report\", \"pr\",             # Phase 4: SCHOLAR sourced research reports, ARTIFICER PR proposals\n16\t})\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef now_iso() -> str:\n20\t    \"\"\"Informational wallclock timestamp. NOT part of the content digest (replay-stable).\"\"\"\n21\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n22\t\n23\t\n24\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n25\tclass SpineRecord:\n26\t    \"\"\"One line of the spine. `cert_digest`/`prev_hash`/`entry_hash` are the chain fields;\n27\t    everything above `ts` is the digested content (ts is informational, excluded from the digest).\"\"\"\n28\t    seq: int\n29\t    scope: str\n30\t    kind: str\n31\t    source: str            # \"claude-code\" | \"git\" | \"doc\" | ...\n32\t    actor: str             # \"user\" | \"assistant\" | session id | commit author | ...\n33\t    payload: dict[str, Any]\n34\t    parent_id: int | None\n35\t    supersedes_id: int | None\n36\t    ts: str\n37\t    cert_digest: str\n38\t    prev_hash: str\n39\t    entry_hash: str\n40\t\n41\t    @classmethod\n42\t    def from_dict(cls, d: dict[str, Any]) -> \"SpineRecord\":\n43\t        return cls(\n44\t            seq=d[\"seq\"], scope=d[\"scope\"], kind=d[\"kind\"], source=d[\"source\"],\n45\t            actor=d[\"actor\"], payload=d.get(\"payload\") or {},\n46\t            parent_id=d.get(\"parent_id\"), supersedes_id=d.get(\"supersedes_id\"),\n47\t            ts=d.get(\"ts\", \"\"), cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"],\n48\t            prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"],\n49\t        )\n50\t\n51\t    def text(self) -> str:\n52\t        \"\"\"Best-effort human/searchable text of the record (for embedding + display).\"\"\"\n53\t        p = self.payload or {}\n54\t        for key in (\"text\", \"content\", \"message\", \"summary\", \"body\", \"title\"):\n55\t            v = p.get(key)\n56\t            if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():\n57\t                return v\n58\t        # tool records / structured payloads: compact JSON fallback\n59\t        import json\n60\t        return json.dumps(p, ensure_ascii=False)[:4000]\n61\t"}]},"uuid":"102fac47-bd33-4bb4-95ce-f5b7530b412f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:37:08.258Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1097b038-5a98-4b86-9a28-12e9f546314a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"20409dce-c472-4336-94e0-1036a4006400","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DwuAxDnFJNFmVkdZNhMJHc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Frontier-vision EGRESS gate (Phase 7, WS-A V-ii). Sending a screen/camera frame to the frontier\n2\tAnthropic VLM uploads private bytes off the owned machine — that is A2 data-egress, not a free\n3\t\"try local then frontier\" quality bump. This gate makes the cascade doctrine-honest:\n4\t\n5\t  • The tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`KernelClassifier` on `vision.frontier.upload` → A2),\n6\t    never self-declared.\n7\t  • Nothing uploads until a VERIFIED owner approval exists that is BOUND to this exact egress — an\n8\t    `egress_token = sha256(frame.sha256 | question)`. An approval of a different egress (a replay)\n9\t    does not match `target_seq` and does not authorize the upload.\n10\t  • Absent approval, the egress is QUEUED (an A2 proposal recorded on the spine) and the frontier\n11\t    reading is WITHHELD; the local (on-box) reading still stands.\n12\t\n13\tReuses the hardened signed `ApprovalQueue` verification (`verify_approval`) — the same fail-closed\n14\towner-key check the mesh already trusts.\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\tEGRESS_SIGNAL = \"vision.egress\"\n22\tFRONTIER_TOOL = \"vision.frontier.upload\"   # classified A2 by the WARDEN oracle (contains \"upload\")\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef egress_token(frame_sha256: str, question: str) -> str:\n26\t    \"\"\"A stable id binding an approval to exactly this (frame, question) upload.\"\"\"\n27\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{frame_sha256}|{question or ''}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef egress_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n31\t    \"\"\"True iff `seq` is an egress request bound to `token` AND carries a VERIFIED owner approval.\n32\t    Fail-closed: wrong token, no approval, denied, or an approval of a different seq → False.\"\"\"\n33\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n34\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != EGRESS_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"egress_token\") != token:\n35\t        return False\n36\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n37\t        p = r.payload\n38\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n39\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey)):\n40\t            return True\n41\t    return False\n42\t"}]},"uuid":"20e7ea0e-e62c-4fe5-bbba-fe274c03dc9e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:37:45.967Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"20409dce-c472-4336-94e0-1036a4006400","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"7ff8f731-ab5a-4787-9b80-9dbb3d11b2e6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012Jsm77KkHQw62gbMeVo1SL","type":"tool_result","content":"200\t            res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n201\t            res.notes.append(\"REFUSED plan: out-of-scope step(s) — nothing recorded as executable\")\n202\t            return PreviewReport(False, \"\", \"A3\", previews, \"refused: out-of-scope\"), res\n203\t        plan_hash = self._plan_hash(previews)\n204\t        plan_tier = max((self._effective_tier(p) for p in previews), default=Tier.A0)\n205\t        # executable plan (WITH content) → 0700 journal, keyed by hash; NEVER the append-only spine\n206\t        _secure(_OP_HOME); _secure(_PLANS)\n207\t        (_PLANS / f\"{plan_hash}.json\").write_text(\n208\t            json.dumps({\"plan_hash\": plan_hash, \"steps\": [asdict(s) for s in steps]}), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n209\t        # spine record: REDACTED — hashes only, no file bytes (BLOCK-7)\n210\t        redacted = [{\"op\": s.op, \"path\": s.path, \"argv\": s.argv, \"cwd\": s.cwd,\n211\t                     \"content_sha256\": (_hash_bytes((s.content or \"\").encode()) if s.op == \"write\" else None)}\n212\t                    for s in steps]\n213\t        res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"operation\", {\n214\t            \"signal\": \"operator.plan\", \"subject\": subject or \"operator plan\",\n215\t            \"summary\": f\"plan: {len(steps)} step(s), tier {plan_tier.label()}, hash {plan_hash[:12]}\",\n216\t            \"plan_hash\": plan_hash, \"plan_tier\": plan_tier.label(), \"plan_journal\": str(_PLANS / f\"{plan_hash}.json\"),\n217\t            \"steps_redacted\": redacted,\n218\t            \"previews\": [{\"op\": p.step[\"op\"], \"path\": p.step.get(\"path\"), \"tool_token\": p.tool_token,\n219\t                          \"tier\": p.tier, \"reversible\": p.reversible, \"pre_hash\": p.pre_hash,\n220\t                          \"post_hash\": p.post_hash} for p in previews],\n221\t        }, plan_tier)])\n222\t        rep = PreviewReport(True, plan_hash, plan_tier.label(), previews, \"previewed (no mutation)\")\n223\t        rep.plan_seq = res.applied[0] if res.applied else (res.queued[0][\"seq\"] if res.queued else None)\n224\t        return rep, res\n225\t\n226\t    # --- B5: execute --------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n227\t    def _approved(self, plan_seq: int) -> bool:\n228\t        from .approvals import SIGNAL as APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n229\t        from .approvals import verify_approval\n230\t        tp = self._tp()\n231\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=plan_seq):\n232\t            p = r.payload\n233\t            if (p.get(\"signal\") == APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == plan_seq\n234\t                    and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, tp)):\n235\t                return True\n236\t        return False\n237\t\n238\t    def execute(self, plan_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n239\t        rec = self.store.get(plan_seq)\n240\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n241\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != \"operator.plan\":\n242\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {plan_seq} is not an operator plan\")\n243\t            return res\n244\t        try:\n245\t            steps = [Step(**s) for s in json.loads(Path(rec.payload[\"plan_journal\"]).read_text())[\"steps\"]]\n246\t        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n247\t            res.notes.append(\"plan journal missing/unreadable — cannot execute\")\n248\t            return res\n249\t        fresh = [self._preview_step(s) for s in steps]\n250\t        # ANTI-TOCTOU: the plan hash (which binds steps + pre/post images) must still match the record.\n251\t        if any(not p.in_scope for p in fresh) or self._plan_hash(fresh) != rec.payload.get(\"plan_hash\"):\n252\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n253\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": plan_seq,\n254\t                                       \"reason\": \"plan hash changed since preview (file changed / out-of-scope) — aborted\"})\n255\t            res.notes.append(\"ABORTED: the world changed since preview (hash mismatch) — nothing executed\")\n256\t            return res\n257\t        # BLOCK-2: RE-DERIVE the tier from the hash-bound previews; never trust the stored plan_tier field.\n258\t        plan_tier = max((self._effective_tier(p) for p in fresh), default=Tier.A0)\n259\t        if plan_tier in (Tier.A2, Tier.A3) and not self._approved(plan_seq):"}]},"uuid":"413d1712-717a-410a-a309-68c7d32549d2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:37:46.984Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7ff8f731-ab5a-4787-9b80-9dbb3d11b2e6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"78d4892b-297e-423f-a549-7bd7fa6c2f0f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA62jhbYJSDLccNPT1Jt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Let me read the governor base dispatch and the agent base to see whether leaving the pending queue triggers execution (which would make a device approval load-bearing)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4290,"cache_read_input_tokens":64255,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4290,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA62hvSb3LSa8D2YQ4YV","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"7dfcda1d-7c16-4a9b-a809-2df1c55c2118","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:37:58.333Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"7dfcda1d-7c16-4a9b-a809-2df1c55c2118","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA62jhbYJSDLccNPT1Jt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013FPFB2V2MYsXp5CyzfQp7k","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/base.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4290,"cache_read_input_tokens":64255,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4290,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA62hvSb3LSa8D2YQ4YV","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"904437fd-29bb-4942-b7be-d25f6c007753","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:37:59.241Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"904437fd-29bb-4942-b7be-d25f6c007753","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013FPFB2V2MYsXp5CyzfQp7k","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"2bd89bca-58bb-4107-8ab5-81ceb48a80d5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:37:59.258Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"904437fd-29bb-4942-b7be-d25f6c007753","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"015e0595-93f1-4ed5-be95-75a98c48cc51","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA63twkM3p5jMrWqkMHJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Wc35N6JF8YgyvnfUBgk2Vz","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/core.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2895,"cache_read_input_tokens":68545,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2895,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA63stFMzaeM82u1Ufvy","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"57381aaf-0592-4e24-a65c-d1db9338c33d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:38:09.293Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"57381aaf-0592-4e24-a65c-d1db9338c33d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Wc35N6JF8YgyvnfUBgk2Vz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The Governor (SIGIL §5) — the single decision point the agent dispatch gate consults for EVERY\n2\tproposal. It composes the kill switch, budgets, and promotion policy into one fail-closed verdict:\n3\tAUTO (write it), QUEUE (hold for human approval), or DENY (refuse + log). The order matters and is\n4\tdeliberately conservative: observe is always allowed; a kill halts everything above observe; budget\n5\tis checked before any auto-grant; A2 auto-approves only under an explicit promotion; A3 never autos.\n6\t\n7\tKill/promotion are read FRESH per decision (a mid-run kill halts the rest of the run). Backward\n8\tcompatible: with no kill, no cap, and no promotion, A0/A1≤ceiling → AUTO and A2/A3 → QUEUE, exactly\n9\tas the pre-Phase-6 gate behaved.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n14\tfrom enum import Enum\n15\tfrom typing import Optional\n16\t\n17\tfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger\n18\tfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch\n19\tfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass Outcome(Enum):\n23\t    AUTO = \"auto\"\n24\t    QUEUE = \"queue\"\n25\t    DENY = \"deny\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n29\tclass Decision:\n30\t    outcome: Outcome\n31\t    reason: str\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef _utc_day() -> str:\n35\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()   # runtime bucketing only (never digested)\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tdef _load_caps() -> BudgetCaps:\n39\t    \"\"\"Optional per-agent caps from ~/.sigil/budgets.json ({daily_actions, daily_interrupts}).\n40\t    Absent/malformed → uncapped (budgets opt-in; default behavior unchanged).\"\"\"\n41\t    try:\n42\t        import json\n43\t\n44\t        from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n45\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"budgets.json\"\n46\t        if not f.exists():\n47\t            return BudgetCaps()\n48\t        d = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n49\t        return BudgetCaps(daily_actions=d.get(\"daily_actions\"), daily_interrupts=d.get(\"daily_interrupts\"))\n50\t    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\n51\t        return BudgetCaps()\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tclass Governor:\n55\t    def __init__(self, store, *, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None, day_iso: Optional[str] = None,\n56\t                 owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n57\t        from .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n58\t        self.store = store\n59\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n60\t        tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n61\t        self.kill = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n62\t        self.promo = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n63\t        self.budget = BudgetLedger(store, caps if caps is not None else _load_caps())\n64\t        self._day = day_iso\n65\t\n66\t    def decide(self, *, agent: str, tier, ceiling, scope: Optional[str] = None) -> Decision:\n67\t        from ..agents.base import AUTO_BAR, Tier\n68\t        scope = scope or \"*\"\n69\t\n70\t        # A0 observe/read is ALWAYS allowed — even under a kill switch (perception + memory stay alive).\n71\t        if tier <= Tier.A0:\n72\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, \"observe (A0)\")\n73\t\n74\t        # KILL SWITCH: the mesh is halted; nothing above observe may act.\n75\t        if self.kill.is_engaged():\n76\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, \"kill switch engaged — agent mesh halted (A0 observe only)\")\n77\t\n78\t        # BUDGET: fail-closed — at cap, deny.\n79\t        over, why = self.budget.over_budget(agent, self._day or _utc_day())\n80\t        if over:\n81\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, why)\n82\t\n83\t        # Within the auto bar (A0/A1) and the agent's ceiling → auto, as always.\n84\t        if tier <= AUTO_BAR and tier <= ceiling:\n85\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"within auto bar ({tier.label()})\")\n86\t\n87\t        # A2 may auto-approve ONLY under an explicit per-scope promotion (never for ENVOY).\n88\t        if tier == Tier.A2 and tier <= ceiling and self.promo.is_promoted(agent, scope):\n89\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"A2 auto-approved by promotion ({agent}/{scope})\")\n90\t\n91\t        # Everything else (A2 unpromoted, A3, or above ceiling) queues for explicit human approval.\n92\t        return Decision(Outcome.QUEUE, f\"queued for approval ({tier.label()})\")\n93\t"}]},"uuid":"d605e41c-9b5d-4bfd-a38b-a3ba89520e5a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:38:09.330Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"57381aaf-0592-4e24-a65c-d1db9338c33d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"d605e41c-9b5d-4bfd-a38b-a3ba89520e5a","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA63twkM3p5jMrWqkMHJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_016s7geyoPxpUsjscEdDj4Jj","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/promotion.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2895,"cache_read_input_tokens":68545,"output_tokens":198,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2895},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":198,"cache_read_input_tokens":68545,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2895,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2895,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA63stFMzaeM82u1Ufvy","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"23796496-5002-46a6-863d-1591717ace3c","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:38:09.578Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"23796496-5002-46a6-863d-1591717ace3c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016s7geyoPxpUsjscEdDj4Jj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-scope promotion policy (SIGIL §5). An owner may promote a specific (agent, scope) so its A2\n2\tproposals auto-approve instead of queuing — EXCEPT ENVOY, which has NO promotion path (§4.6). A3\n3\tnever promotes.\n4\t\n5\tAUTHENTICATED (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2/RP-4): a grant/revoke is meaningless unless SIGNED by the owner\n6\tkey and verified against the persisted trusted pubkey. `is_promoted` ignores any grant that does not\n7\tverify — so a forged grant written by a prompt-injected agent via `self.store` grants nothing. The\n8\tgate scope is the proposal's RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label\n9\t(RP-3), so a promotion is \"agent X may auto-approve A2 records of kind K.\" \"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n15\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n16\t\n17\tSIGNAL = \"governor.promotion\"\n18\tNO_PROMOTION_AGENTS = frozenset({\"ENVOY\"})   # structural: outbound stays human-gated forever (§4.6)\n19\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"agent\", \"scope\")\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass PromotionPolicy:\n23\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n24\t        self.store = store\n25\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n26\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n27\t\n28\t    def grant(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\"):\n29\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n30\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\",\n31\t                              payload={\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"agent\": agent,\n32\t                                       \"scope\": scope, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n33\t                                       \"reason\": f\"{agent} has no promotion path (SIGIL §4.6) — outbound stays human-gated\"})\n34\t            return None\n35\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"granted\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n36\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n37\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n38\t\n39\t    def revoke(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> int:\n40\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n41\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n42\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n43\t\n44\t    def is_promoted(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> bool:\n45\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n46\t            return False                          # never — structural, not policy\n47\t        state: dict = {}\n48\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n49\t            p = r.payload\n50\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL or p.get(\"state\") not in (\"granted\", \"revoked\"):\n51\t                continue\n52\t            if not verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n53\t                continue                          # fail-closed: an unsigned/forged grant grants nothing\n54\t            state[(p.get(\"agent\"), p.get(\"scope\"))] = p[\"state\"]   # latest verified wins\n55\t        return state.get((agent, scope)) == \"granted\" or state.get((agent, \"*\")) == \"granted\"\n56\t"}]},"uuid":"2636d0f4-e49d-4796-b851-ef0eec6a0223","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:38:09.597Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"23796496-5002-46a6-863d-1591717ace3c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Run: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python tests/test_mobile.py\"\"\"\n4\timport sys\n5\timport tempfile\n6\t\n7\tfrom sigil.agents.approvals import pending, verify_approval\n8\tfrom sigil.agents.base import Agent, Proposal, Tier\n9\tfrom sigil.bridge import BridgeDaemon, PushNotifier, bind_ok\n10\tfrom sigil.governor import Governor\n11\tfrom sigil.mesh import (DeviceApprover, advertise_capability, authorize_device,\n12\t                        authorized_devices, capability_map, revoke_device)\n13\tfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\n14\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n15\t\n16\tOWNER = generate_keypair()\n17\tOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tdef _store():\n21\t    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tclass _Emitter(Agent):\n25\t    name = \"TESTER\"\n26\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n27\t\n28\t    def __init__(self, store):\n29\t        super().__init__(store, governor=Governor(store, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey=OP))\n30\t\n31\t    def run(self, tier, kind=\"draft\"):\n32\t        return self._dispatch([Proposal(kind, {\"subject\": \"TOP SECRET wire $1M\"}, tier)])\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t# ---- D1 platform backend selection ---------------------------------------------------------------\n36\tdef test_host_selects_backend_by_platform(monkeypatch=None):\n37\t    import sigil.platform as P\n38\t    real = sys.platform\n39\t    try:\n40\t        sys.platform = \"linux\"; assert type(P.host()).__name__ == \"LinuxBackend\"\n41\t        sys.platform = \"darwin\"; assert type(P.host()).__name__ == \"MacOSBackend\"\n42\t        sys.platform = \"win32\"; assert type(P.host()).__name__ == \"WindowsBackend\"\n43\t    finally:\n44\t        sys.platform = real\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef test_linux_backend_delegates_and_probes_honestly():\n48\t    import sigil.platform.linux as L\n49\t    calls = {\"screen\": 0}\n50\t    L.grab_screen = lambda: (calls.__setitem__(\"screen\", 1) or None)\n51\t    b = L.LinuxBackend()\n52\t    assert b.capture_screen() is None and calls[\"screen\"] == 1, \"Linux backend delegates to grab_screen\"\n53\t    caps = b.capabilities()\n54\t    assert caps.os == \"linux\" and isinstance(caps.has_screen, bool), \"capabilities are honest booleans\"\n55\t\n56\t\n57\t# ---- D2 secrets: keyring-first, never on the spine -----------------------------------------------\n58\tdef test_secret_store_roundtrips_and_env_fallback():\n59\t    from sigil.platform.secrets import SecretStore\n60\t    import os\n61\t    s = SecretStore()\n62\t    s.set(\"SIGIL_TEST_SECRET\", \"hunter2\")\n63\t    assert s.get(\"SIGIL_TEST_SECRET\") == \"hunter2\", \"a stored secret round-trips\"\n64\t    os.environ.pop(\"SIGIL_TEST_SECRET\", None)\n65\t\n66\t\n67\t# ---- D4 device-authorization ledger (the keystone) -----------------------------------------------\n68\tdef test_device_approves_only_while_authorized():\n69\t    s = _store()\n70\t    _Emitter(s).run(Tier.A2)                                   # queued A2 at seq 0\n71\t    tgt = pending(s, OP)[0].seq\n72\t    device = generate_keypair()\n73\t    # (a) an UNAUTHORIZED device approval does NOT resolve the item\n74\t    DeviceApprover(s, device_key=device).approve(tgt)\n75\t    assert len(pending(s, OP, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(s, OP))) == 1, \"an unauthorized device can't approve\"\n76\t    # (b) owner AUTHORIZES the device → a device approval now resolves it\n77\t    authorize_device(s, \"phone-1\", device.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n78\t    assert device.public_key_b64 in authorized_devices(s, OP)\n79\t    DeviceApprover(s, device_key=device).approve(tgt)\n80\t    assert not pending(s, OP, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(s, OP)), \"an authorized device approval verifies + clears\"\n81\t\n82\t\n83\tdef test_forged_and_revoked_device_cannot_approve():\n84\t    s = _store()\n85\t    _Emitter(s).run(Tier.A3)\n86\t    tgt = pending(s, OP)[0].seq\n87\t    # a device that FORGES an authorization for itself (signed with its OWN key, not the owner's) → ignored\n88\t    attacker = generate_keypair()\n89\t    from sigil.governor.authn import signed_payload\n90\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n91\t             payload=signed_payload({\"signal\": \"mesh.device\", \"state\": \"authorized\",\n92\t                                     \"device_id\": \"evil\", \"device_pubkey\": attacker.public_key_b64}, attacker))\n93\t    assert attacker.public_key_b64 not in authorized_devices(s, OP), \"a self-signed authorization is not owner-minted\"\n94\t    DeviceApprover(s, device_key=attacker).approve(tgt)\n95\t    assert len(pending(s, OP, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(s, OP))) == 1, \"the forged device can't approve\"\n96\t    # a genuinely authorized device that is later REVOKED loses approval power\n97\t    dev = generate_keypair()\n98\t    authorize_device(s, \"phone-2\", dev.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n99\t    revoke_device(s, \"phone-2\", dev.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n100\t    assert dev.public_key_b64 not in authorized_devices(s, OP), \"a revoked device is no longer authorized\"\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tdef test_device_approval_target_seq_binding_no_replay():\n104\t    s = _store()\n105\t    _Emitter(s).run(Tier.A2, kind=\"draft\")                    # harmless, seq 0\n106\t    _Emitter(s).run(Tier.A3, kind=\"wire\")                     # dangerous, seq 1\n107\t    pend = pending(s, OP)\n108\t    harmless, dangerous = pend[0].seq, pend[1].seq\n109\t    device = generate_keypair()\n110\t    authorize_device(s, \"phone\", device.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n111\t    approved = DeviceApprover(s, device_key=device).approve(harmless)   # approve the HARMLESS one\n112\t    genuine = s.get(approved).payload\n113\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", supersedes_id=dangerous, payload=dict(genuine))  # replay\n114\t    still = [r.seq for r in pending(s, OP, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(s, OP))]\n115\t    assert dangerous in still and harmless not in still, \"a replayed device approval (signed for another seq) can't resolve the dangerous item\"\n116\t\n117\t\n118\t# ---- D3 signed capability map --------------------------------------------------------------------\n119\tdef test_capability_map_ignores_forged_advertisements():\n120\t    s = _store()\n121\t    advertise_capability(s, {\"host_id\": \"desk\", \"os\": \"linux\", \"has_screen\": True, \"has_camera\": True,\n122\t                             \"has_gpu_vlm\": True, \"always_on\": True}, OWNER)\n123\t    attacker = generate_keypair()\n124\t    from sigil.governor.authn import signed_payload\n125\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n126\t             payload=signed_payload({\"signal\": \"mesh.host_capability\", \"host_id\": \"rogue\", \"os\": \"?\",\n127\t                                     \"has_screen\": True, \"has_camera\": True, \"has_gpu_vlm\": True,\n128\t                                     \"always_on\": True}, attacker))\n129\t    m = capability_map(s, OP)\n130\t    assert \"desk\" in m and \"rogue\" not in m, \"only owner-signed capability advertisements are trusted\"\n131\t\n132\t\n133\t# ---- D5/D6 bridge daemon + push -------------------------------------------------------------------\n134\tdef test_bridge_daemon_gates_device_approval():\n135\t    s = _store()\n136\t    _Emitter(s).run(Tier.A2)\n137\t    tgt = pending(s, OP)[0].seq\n138\t    d = BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP)\n139\t    device = generate_keypair()\n140\t    from sigil.agents.approvals import _approval_message\n141\t    from sigil.reuse import sign\n142\t    msg = _approval_message(tgt, \"approved\", \"phone\")\n143\t    forged = {\"signal\": \"governor.approval\", \"approval\": \"approved\", \"target_seq\": tgt, \"approver\": \"phone\",\n144\t              \"pubkey\": device.public_key_b64, \"sig\": sign(device.private_key_b64, msg)}\n145\t    try:\n146\t        d.submit_device_approval(forged); assert False, \"an unauthorized device approval must be refused\"\n147\t    except ValueError:\n148\t        pass\n149\t    authorize_device(s, \"phone\", device.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n150\t    d.submit_device_approval(forged)                          # now authorized → accepted\n151\t    assert not d.pending(), \"an authorized device approval clears the queue via the daemon\"\n152\t\n153\t\n154\tdef test_push_carries_no_subject_or_secret():\n155\t    s = _store()\n156\t    n = PushNotifier(s)                                       # starts at head → only new items\n157\t    _Emitter(s).run(Tier.A2)                                  # queue an A2 with a secret subject\n158\t    _Emitter(s).run(Tier.A1, kind=\"event\")                   # an A1 auto action (must NOT push)\n159\t    pushes = n.poll()\n160\t    assert len(pushes) == 1 and pushes[0][\"tier\"] == \"A2\", \"only A2/A3 queued items push\"\n161\t    blob = str(pushes)\n162\t    assert \"SECRET\" not in blob and \"subject\" not in blob, \"the push carries only {seq,tier,kind} — no subject/secret\"\n163\t\n164\t\n165\tdef test_bind_guard_refuses_public_and_unspecified():\n166\t    assert bind_ok(\"127.0.0.1\") and bind_ok(\"10.13.13.2\") and bind_ok(\"192.168.1.5\")   # loopback / WG / private\n167\t    assert not bind_ok(\"0.0.0.0\") and not bind_ok(\"::\"), \"0.0.0.0 / :: refused\"\n168\t    assert not bind_ok(\"8.8.8.8\") and not bind_ok(\"1.2.3.4\"), \"public addresses refused\"\n169\t\n170\t\n171\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n172\t    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith(\"test_\")]\n173\t    passed = 0\n174\t    for fn in fns:\n175\t        try:\n176\t            fn(); print(f\"  PASS  {fn.__name__}\"); passed += 1\n177\t        except AssertionError as e:\n178\t            print(f\"  FAIL  {fn.__name__}: {e}\")\n179\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n180\t            import traceback\n181\t            print(f\"  ERROR {fn.__name__}: {e}\")\n182\t            traceback.print_exc()\n183\t    print(f\"{passed}/{len(fns)} Phase-7 WS-D (cross-platform + mobile) guarantees hold\")\n184\t"}]},"uuid":"c38e9a4c-d129-4acf-a761-1ea267001a53","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:39:40.521Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b92246be-d04e-4b15-a5c7-a4cfc8cd7979","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"1e2f0d7a-97bc-4149-8850-cd79b2f18a54","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TMFQQDstrE3Da6ij8KnuQ8","type":"tool_result","content":"diff --git a/sigil/cli.py b/sigil/cli.py\nindex 33ccc0c..41cee82 100644\n--- a/sigil/cli.py\n+++ b/sigil/cli.py\n@@ -335,6 +335,14 @@ def cmd_dashboard(a) -> None:\n     print(render_dashboard(snapshot(SpineStore())))\n \n \n+def cmd_host(a) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Show this host's capability descriptor (WS-D) — what the mesh routes on.\"\"\"\n+    from .platform import host\n+    d = host().capabilities()\n+    print(f\"  host {d.host_id} · {d.os}\")\n+    print(f\"    screen={d.has_screen} camera={d.has_camera} gpu_vlm={d.has_gpu_vlm} always_on={d.always_on}\")\n+\n+\n def cmd_serve(a) -> None:\n     \"\"\"Start the loopback glass-cockpit UI. Mints a fresh session token (printed here); every\n     request needs it and no web page can read it. Read plane is GET-only; the owner-signed action\n@@ -463,6 +471,9 @@ def main(argv=None) -> None:\n     psv = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"start the loopback glass-cockpit UI\")\n     psv.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8733)\n     psv.set_defaults(fn=cmd_serve)\n+    ph = sub.add_parser(\"host\", help=\"this host's mesh capability descriptor\")\n+    ph.add_argument(\"action\", nargs=\"?\", default=\"caps\", choices=[\"caps\"])\n+    ph.set_defaults(fn=cmd_host)\n     a = p.parse_args(argv)\n     a.fn(a)\n \n=== bridge/mesh/device refs in cli ===\n295:        print(f\"  kill switch: {'ENGAGED (mesh halted)' if KillSwitch(store).is_engaged() else 'released (mesh live)'}\")\n300:        print(f\"  KILL SWITCH ENGAGED (seq {seq}) — agent mesh halted; perception + memory read stay alive\")\n303:        print(f\"  kill switch released (seq {seq}, owner-signed) — agent mesh live again\")\n339:    \"\"\"Show this host's capability descriptor (WS-D) — what the mesh routes on.\"\"\"\n459:    pau = sub.add_parser(\"audit\", help=\"self-audit (C18): what the mesh did and why, from the log\")\n474:    ph = sub.add_parser(\"host\", help=\"this host's mesh capability descriptor\")","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"a34e7169-e294-4234-a9a1-c31d1f40f33d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:40:58.996Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1e2f0d7a-97bc-4149-8850-cd79b2f18a54","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"1df745fc-0a0f-4b71-a2cd-be2df0eab358","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ffbb8a8a-4bb8-4c99-a79e-9ed4dd97a041","agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NzQJEoRQoUdZrZuWqtXsGm","type":"tool_result","content":"=== venv? ===\n/home/kali/.sigil/venv/bin/python\ncryptography ok\n=== perception/capture top imports ===\n\"\"\"Capture backends (SIGIL §8). A `Frame` is the immutable record of one capture: the image bytes'\nsha256 (integrity), the OCR/accessibility TEXT (the GROUND TRUTH a query is answered from), and a\npath to the image. Real backends shell out to whatever is installed (screenshot tool + tesseract,\nv4l2/ffmpeg); every backend returns `None` on any failure — a missing camera is no capture, never a\nfabricated one (same posture as SCHOLAR.read_source). `StaticFrame` is the deterministic test double.\n\nHONEST GAPS: headless hosts often lack a screenshot tool / tesseract / a camera, so the real\nbackends degrade to `None` or an image-only Frame (text=\"\"). The richest real screen source is the\nOS accessibility tree (C4); it is not available on a headless box and is left as a documented seam.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n\n_SCREENSHOT_TOOLS = (\n    (\"scrot\", [\"scrot\", \"-o\"]),\n    (\"gnome-screenshot\", [\"gnome-screenshot\", \"-f\"]),\n    (\"spectacle\", [\"spectacle\", \"-b\", \"-n\", \"-o\"]),\n    (\"import\", [\"import\", \"-window\", \"root\"]),   # ImageMagick\n)\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Frame:","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"b4c23c11-706e-4232-b8eb-a7a2837984c9","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:41:02.538Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1df745fc-0a0f-4b71-a2cd-be2df0eab358","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"d9b3d12d-9fd7-4325-ad55-8f06016c2e10","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad15b90f46f30dffa","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdA6KaGns71EW8SuWzvyY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01YRfndaHZJcfnAsWz4v7den","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python - <<'PY'\nimport tempfile\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair, sign\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.agents.approvals import pending, verify_approval, _approval_message, SIGNAL\nfrom sigil.agents.base import Agent, Proposal, Tier\nfrom sigil.governor import Governor\nfrom sigil.mesh import DeviceApprover, authorize_device, revoke_device, authorized_devices\nfrom sigil.bridge import BridgeDaemon\n\nOWNER = generate_keypair(); OP = OWNER.public_key_b64\ndef store(): return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\nclass E(Agent):\n    name=\"TESTER\"; ceiling=Tier.A2\n    def __init__(s,st): super().__init__(st, governor=Governor(st, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey=OP))\n    def run(s,t,k=\"draft\"): return s._dispatch([Proposal(k,{\"subject\":\"WIRE $1M\"},t)])\n\nprint(\"=== ATTACK A: strong replay — edit target_seq in a genuine device approval payload ===\")\ns=store(); E(s).run(Tier.A2,\"draft\"); E(s).run(Tier.A3,\"wire\")\npend=pending(s,OP); harmless,dangerous=pend[0].seq,pend[1].seq\ndev=generate_keypair(); authorize_device(s,\"phone\",dev.public_key_b64,OWNER)\nappseq=DeviceApprover(s,device_key=dev).approve(harmless)\ngenuine=dict(s.get(appseq).payload)\ntampered=dict(genuine); tampered[\"target_seq\"]=dangerous   # point sig'd-for-harmless at dangerous\ns.append(kind=\"event\",source=\"mesh\",actor=\"DEVICE\",payload=tampered,supersedes_id=dangerous)\nstill=[r.seq for r in pending(s,OP,extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(s,OP))]\nprint(f\"  pending after tamper: {still}  -> dangerous still pending? {dangerous in still}\")\nassert dangerous in still, \"REPLAY SUCCEEDED\"\nprint(\"  target_seq binding holds (tampered sig fails verify)\")\n\nprint(\"=== ATTACK B: does a DEVICE approval satisfy the OWNER-only operator/egress gates? ===\")\nfrom sigil.agents.approvals import verify_approval as va\ns=store(); E(s).run(Tier.A2,\"draft\"); tgt=pending(s,OP)[0].seq\ndev=generate_keypair(); authorize_device(s,\"phone\",dev.public_key_b64,OWNER)\nDeviceApprover(s,device_key=dev).approve(tgt)\n# emulate operator._approved / egress_approved gate: owner-only verify_approval\nowner_gate=[r for r in s.iter_records()\n            if r.payload.get(\"signal\")==SIGNAL and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\")==tgt\n            and r.payload.get(\"approval\")==\"approved\" and va(r, OP)]  # NO extra_pubkeys\nprint(f\"  owner-only gate counts device approval? {len(owner_gate)>0}  (must be False)\")\n\nprint(\"=== ATTACK C: revoked device via daemon ===\")\ns=store(); E(s).run(Tier.A2); tgt=pending(s,OP)[0].seq\nd=BridgeDaemon(s,trusted_pubkey=OP); dev=generate_keypair()\nauthorize_device(s,\"p\",dev.public_key_b64,OWNER); revoke_device(s,\"p\",dev.public_key_b64,OWNER)\nmsg=_approval_message(tgt,\"approved\",\"phone\")\npayload={\"signal\":SIGNAL,\"approval\":\"approved\",\"target_seq\":tgt,\"approver\":\"phone\",\n         \"pubkey\":dev.public_key_b64,\"sig\":sign(dev.private_key_b64,msg)}\ntry:\n    d.submit_device_approval(payload); print(\"  revoked device ACCEPTED -> BUG\")\nexcept ValueError as e: print(f\"  revoked device refused: {e}\")\n\nprint(\"=== ATTACK D: authorized device dismisses (deny) a dangerous item from phone queue ===\")\ns=store(); E(s).run(Tier.A3,\"wire\"); tgt=pending(s,OP)[0].seq\ndev=generate_keypair(); authorize_device(s,\"p\",dev.public_key_b64,OWNER)\nDeviceApprover(s,device_key=dev).deny(tgt)   # a DENY also resolves\nph=[r.seq for r in pending(s,OP,extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(s,OP))]\nown=[r.seq for r in pending(s,OP)]  # owner dashboard view (no extra_pubkeys)\nprint(f\"  phone view pending={ph}  owner-dashboard view pending={own}\")\n\nprint(\"=== ATTACK E: macos/windows backends import cleanly on Linux ===\")\nimport importlib\nfor m in (\"sigil.platform.macos\",\"sigil.platform.windows\"):\n    importlib.import_module(m); print(f\"  imported {m} OK\")\nPY","description":"Empirical keystone attacks: replay, owner-gate isolation, revoke, dismiss, imports"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1541,"cache_read_input_tokens":92658,"output_tokens":2467,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1541},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2467,"cache_read_input_tokens":92658,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1541,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1541,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdA6KYsTEbb3s5MDL8azj","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"4ced3bc8-3527-4f80-a005-c93916fe3bab","timestamp":"2026-07-18T19:42:08.550Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
